Gold (XAUUSD / COMEX GC): astrological windows, 20 Nov 2026 – 31 Dec 2027

Every rule in the two reference PDFs, the user notes (Reference_materials/user_notes_gold.md) and in the 2026 document that names gold, recomputed (Swiss Ephemeris, sidereal Lahiri, mean node). Times are US Eastern (America/New_York: EST, shown as EDT 14 Mar–7 Nov 2027). Results are quoted from the sources; they are the sources' claims, not forecasts or investment advice.

Month by month (conditions active at any time in the month)

A count of active conditions, not a net forecast: the sources give no weighting between rules, and TA p.30 says longer multi-planet combinations dominate single ones.

1Sat 01 Aug 2026 17:43 EDTSat 10 Apr 2027 08:22 EDTRahu in Dhanishta (market and bullion risk)
Rahu in Dhanishta nakshatra.
Conditions & qualifications (4)
  • KD prints the window as '2 August to year end' (2026); the configuration itself lasts until Rahu leaves Dhanishta, which is the window computed here.
  • KD p.111: 'Rahu's vedh of Vishaka supports stability' for banking and finance; KD p.113: 'Jupiter trine may prevent' a general fall.
  • KD p.111 bottom: 'Trading guidance: exercise extreme caution'.
  • Rahu/Ketu sign/nakshatra changes: the 2026 document's almanac prints the 5 Dec 2026 node ingress 40 min earlier (21:52 IST) than the mean node computed here (22:32 IST) - a known 5-arcsecond formulation gap (config/conventions.yaml).
Bearish
Market and bullion: negative outlook ('Rahu in Dhanistha, ruled by Mars (enemy), is bearish'; 'High probability of adverse moves')
Rahu 6.67 deg Aquarius (Dhanishta p4) | DhanishtaKD p.111 'Rahu in Dhanistha: Market and Bullion Risk' | KD p.113 'Rahu in Dhanistha: Negative for Markets and Bullion'start = astronomy-layer nakshatra ingress of Rahu ✓
end = astronomy-layer nakshatra ingress of Rahu ✓
2Sun 16 Aug 2026 22:29 EDTThu 14 Jan 2027 10:40 ESTSun in Leo: store gold and silver, sell in the 5th month (Brihat Samhita 42.6)
Buy when the Sun enters Simha; sell in the 5th solar month (Sun in Dhanus).
Conditions & qualifications (3)
  • 2000-2025 test (25 years): gold up by mid-December in 14 and by mid-January in 18, while 66% of all same-length holds rose; silver 14 and 17. No edge over the normal drift.
  • 42.13: the profit 'depends upon ... the Sun or the Moon ... being conjoined with very friendly planets and aspected by planets who are very friendly' - not applied (the friendship table to use is not given).
  • Effect timing per source: Sell in the 5th month (Sun in Sagittarius, about mid-December to mid-January)
Bullish
Profit on gold and silver (Bullish): 'If one stores up gold, gems, skins, armours, weapons, pearls and silver when the Sun is in Simha, and sell them in the 5th month, he will get profit and sustain loss otherwise'
Sun 0.00 deg Leo (Magha p1) | buy: Sun enters Simha; 5th month begins utc: 2026-12-16 04:55:18+00:00; sell by: Sun enters Makara (5th month ends)Brihat Samhita, tr. P.S. Sastri 1946, Ch.42 sl.6 (PDF p.332)start = astronomy-layer sign ingress of Sun ✓

3Fri 09 Oct 2026 11:22 EDTSun 07 Feb 2027 21:21 ESTSaturn in Uttara Bhadrapada (Brihat Samhita 10.17)
Saturn in Uttara Bhadrapada nakshatra (333deg20' - 346deg40' sidereal).
Conditions & qualifications (2)
  • Recurs every ~29.5 years; 2000-2026 holds one passage in three windows: 28 Apr - 3 Oct 2025 gold +17%, 20 Jan - 17 May 2026 -0.7%, 9 Oct 2026 - 8 Feb 2027 (current) -15% to 20 Nov 2026.
  • Same verse: Saturn in Satabhishak and Purvabhadra makes 'traders' suffer (general market, not gold).
Bearish
Gold afflicted (Bearish): '... so do those living on the banks of rivers, cart-wrights, women and gold when he is in Uttarabhadra'

4Thu 19 Nov 2026 16:34 ESTWed 02 Dec 2026 20:58 ESTSun in a Saturn or Rahu nakshatra (user note)
Sun in Pushya, Anuradha, Uttara Bhadrapada (Saturn) or Ardra, Swati, Shatabhisha (Rahu).
Conditions & qualifications (3)
  • Gold is ruled by the Sun (user note; TA S29 lists no gold sector, the PDFs' gold rules are mostly Sun placements).
  • Conflict: TA p.155 S34 A10 lists Sun on Bharani, Krittika, Punarvasu and Pushya as gold-RALLY stars (Pushya is a Saturn nakshatra), 'not aspected by Benefic - More Bullish if aspected by Malefic'; TA p.156 S34 B1: Sun on Anuradha (Saturn nakshatra) with or aspected by a benefic - gold can fall. Both sources are shown.
  • User note: 'fear, greed and market illusions are Rahu's territory'.
Bearish
Can trigger fall in gold prices (Bearish)
Sun 3.33 deg Scorpio (Anuradha p1) | Sun in Anuradha (lord Saturn)User note (project chat, 20 Nov 2026; Reference_materials/user_notes_gold.md)start = astronomy-layer nakshatra ingress of Sun ✓
end = astronomy-layer nakshatra ingress of Sun ✓
5Mon 23 Nov 2026 20:54 ESTTue 24 Nov 2026 09:53 ESTVenus, Mercury, Jupiter or waxing Moon with / aspecting the Sun (user note)
Venus, Mercury or Jupiter in the Sun's sign or casting drishti on it, or the Moon doing so during Shukla paksha.
Conditions & qualifications (3)
  • Aspect = whole-sign graha drishti (Jupiter 5/7/9, Saturn 3/7/10, Rahu 7 - project convention; Rahu's 7th falls on Ketu's sign); conjunction = same sign. Venus and Mercury can only conjoin the Sun (they never get far enough to aspect it).
  • Conflict: TA p.154 S34 A3 and the 2026 document's gold-bullish #2 make the Sun in Libra with/aspected by malefics (Saturn, Mars, Rahu) BULLISH for gold, and TA S34 A10 says a malefic aspect makes the Sun's rally stars MORE bullish; TA S34 B1 makes a benefic with the Sun on Anuradha bearish. Both sources are shown.
  • Mercury is never more than ~28 deg from the Sun, so it shares the Sun's sign for long stretches of the year.

  • Mercury is never more than ~28 deg from the Sun, so it shares the Sun's sign for long stretches of the year.

41Thu 21 Jan 2027 18:40 ESTFri 22 Jan 2027 07:17 ESTVenus, Mercury, Jupiter or waxing Moon with / aspecting the Sun (user note)
Venus, Mercury or Jupiter in the Sun's sign or casting drishti on it, or the Moon doing so during Shukla paksha.
Conditions & qualifications (3)
  • Aspect = whole-sign graha drishti (Jupiter 5/7/9, Saturn 3/7/10, Rahu 7 - project convention; Rahu's 7th falls on Ketu's sign); conjunction = same sign. Venus and Mercury can only conjoin the Sun (they never get far enough to aspect it).
  • Conflict: TA p.154 S34 A3 and the 2026 document's gold-bullish #2 make the Sun in Libra with/aspected by malefics (Saturn, Mars, Rahu) BULLISH for gold, and TA S34 A10 says a malefic aspect makes the Sun's rally stars MORE bullish; TA S34 B1 makes a benefic with the Sun on Anuradha bearish. Both sources are shown.
  • Mercury is never more than ~28 deg from the Sun, so it shares the Sun's sign for long stretches of the year.

55Mon 08 Feb 2027Moon darshan (first sighting) on Monday or Friday
First evening sighting after the new moon falls on a Monday or Friday.
Conditions & qualifications (2)
  • 'Moon rise' / 'Moon darshan' read as the first evening sighting after Amavasya (Chandra Darshan). Operational criterion (not in either PDF): first New York sunset with elongation >= 12 deg and Moon age >= 20 h. Indian almanacs date the darshan for Indian sunset and can be a day earlier.
  • The 2026 document's two printed rows (Fri 20-Mar-26, Mon 12-Oct-26) are both darshan evenings, which is how 'moonrise' is read here.

83Wed 07 Apr 2027 08:56 EDTWed 14 Apr 2027 05:58 EDTVenus, Mercury, Jupiter or waxing Moon with / aspecting the Sun (user note)
Venus, Mercury or Jupiter in the Sun's sign or casting drishti on it, or the Moon doing so during Shukla paksha.
Conditions & qualifications (3)
  • Aspect = whole-sign graha drishti (Jupiter 5/7/9, Saturn 3/7/10, Rahu 7 - project convention; Rahu's 7th falls on Ketu's sign); conjunction = same sign. Venus and Mercury can only conjoin the Sun (they never get far enough to aspect it).
  • Conflict: TA p.154 S34 A3 and the 2026 document's gold-bullish #2 make the Sun in Libra with/aspected by malefics (Saturn, Mars, Rahu) BULLISH for gold, and TA S34 A10 says a malefic aspect makes the Sun's rally stars MORE bullish; TA S34 B1 makes a benefic with the Sun on Anuradha bearish. Both sources are shown.
  • Mercury is never more than ~28 deg from the Sun, so it shares the Sun's sign for long stretches of the year.

94Fri 07 May 2027Moon darshan (first sighting) on Monday or Friday
First evening sighting after the new moon falls on a Monday or Friday.
Conditions & qualifications (2)
  • 'Moon rise' / 'Moon darshan' read as the first evening sighting after Amavasya (Chandra Darshan). Operational criterion (not in either PDF): first New York sunset with elongation >= 12 deg and Moon age >= 20 h. Indian almanacs date the darshan for Indian sunset and can be a day earlier.
  • The 2026 document's two printed rows (Fri 20-Mar-26, Mon 12-Oct-26) are both darshan evenings, which is how 'moonrise' is read here.

  • Conflict: TA p.154 S34 A3 and the 2026 document's gold-bullish #2 make the Sun in Libra with/aspected by malefics (Saturn, Mars, Rahu) BULLISH for gold, and TA S34 A10 says a malefic aspect makes the Sun's rally stars MORE bullish; TA S34 B1 makes a benefic with the Sun on Anuradha bearish. Both sources are shown.
  • Mercury is never more than ~28 deg from the Sun, so it shares the Sun's sign for long stretches of the year.

131Thu 22 Jul 2027 16:17 EDTFri 30 Jul 2027 09:01 EDTVenus, Mercury, Jupiter or waxing Moon with / aspecting the Sun (user note)
Venus, Mercury or Jupiter in the Sun's sign or casting drishti on it, or the Moon doing so during Shukla paksha.
Conditions & qualifications (3)
  • Aspect = whole-sign graha drishti (Jupiter 5/7/9, Saturn 3/7/10, Rahu 7 - project convention; Rahu's 7th falls on Ketu's sign); conjunction = same sign. Venus and Mercury can only conjoin the Sun (they never get far enough to aspect it).
  • Conflict: TA p.154 S34 A3 and the 2026 document's gold-bullish #2 make the Sun in Libra with/aspected by malefics (Saturn, Mars, Rahu) BULLISH for gold, and TA S34 A10 says a malefic aspect makes the Sun's rally stars MORE bullish; TA S34 B1 makes a benefic with the Sun on Anuradha bearish. Both sources are shown.
  • Mercury is never more than ~28 deg from the Sun, so it shares the Sun's sign for long stretches of the year.

144Tue 17 Aug 2027 04:44 EDTFri 14 Jan 2028 16:54 ESTSun in Leo: store gold and silver, sell in the 5th month (Brihat Samhita 42.6)
Buy when the Sun enters Simha; sell in the 5th solar month (Sun in Dhanus).
Conditions & qualifications (3)
  • 2000-2025 test (25 years): gold up by mid-December in 14 and by mid-January in 18, while 66% of all same-length holds rose; silver 14 and 17. No edge over the normal drift.
  • 42.13: the profit 'depends upon ... the Sun or the Moon ... being conjoined with very friendly planets and aspected by planets who are very friendly' - not applied (the friendship table to use is not given).
  • Effect timing per source: Sell in the 5th month (Sun in Sagittarius, about mid-December to mid-January)

  • Conflict: TA p.154 S34 A3 and the 2026 document's gold-bullish #2 make the Sun in Libra with/aspected by malefics (Saturn, Mars, Rahu) BULLISH for gold, and TA S34 A10 says a malefic aspect makes the Sun's rally stars MORE bullish; TA S34 B1 makes a benefic with the Sun on Anuradha bearish. Both sources are shown.
  • Mercury is never more than ~28 deg from the Sun, so it shares the Sun's sign for long stretches of the year.

163Fri 01 Oct 2027Moon darshan (first sighting) on Monday or Friday
First evening sighting after the new moon falls on a Monday or Friday.
Conditions & qualifications (2)
  • 'Moon rise' / 'Moon darshan' read as the first evening sighting after Amavasya (Chandra Darshan). Operational criterion (not in either PDF): first New York sunset with elongation >= 12 deg and Moon age >= 20 h. Indian almanacs date the darshan for Indian sunset and can be a day earlier.
  • The 2026 document's two printed rows (Fri 20-Mar-26, Mon 12-Oct-26) are both darshan evenings, which is how 'moonrise' is read here.

  • 'Moon rise' / 'Moon darshan' read as the first evening sighting after Amavasya (Chandra Darshan). Operational criterion (not in either PDF): first New York sunset with elongation >= 12 deg and Moon age >= 20 h. Indian almanacs date the darshan for Indian sunset and can be a day earlier.

175Fri 05 Nov 2027 07:41 EDTThu 11 Nov 2027 21:26 ESTVenus, Mercury, Jupiter or waxing Moon with / aspecting the Sun (user note)
Venus, Mercury or Jupiter in the Sun's sign or casting drishti on it, or the Moon doing so during Shukla paksha.
Conditions & qualifications (3)
  • Aspect = whole-sign graha drishti (Jupiter 5/7/9, Saturn 3/7/10, Rahu 7 - project convention; Rahu's 7th falls on Ketu's sign); conjunction = same sign. Venus and Mercury can only conjoin the Sun (they never get far enough to aspect it).
  • Conflict: TA p.154 S34 A3 and the 2026 document's gold-bullish #2 make the Sun in Libra with/aspected by malefics (Saturn, Mars, Rahu) BULLISH for gold, and TA S34 A10 says a malefic aspect makes the Sun's rally stars MORE bullish; TA S34 B1 makes a benefic with the Sun on Anuradha bearish. Both sources are shown.
  • Mercury is never more than ~28 deg from the Sun, so it shares the Sun's sign for long stretches of the year.

  • Aspect = whole-sign graha drishti (Jupiter 5/7/9, Saturn 3/7/10, Rahu 7 - project convention; Rahu's 7th falls on Ketu's sign); conjunction = same sign. Venus and Mercury can only conjoin the Sun (they never get far enough to aspect it).
  • Conflict: TA p.154 S34 A3 and the 2026 document's gold-bullish #2 make the Sun in Libra with/aspected by malefics (Saturn, Mars, Rahu) BULLISH for gold, and TA S34 A10 says a malefic aspect makes the Sun's rally stars MORE bullish; TA S34 B1 makes a benefic with the Sun on Anuradha bearish. Both sources are shown.
  • Mercury is never more than ~28 deg from the Sun, so it shares the Sun's sign for long stretches of the year.

Your notes on the Sun (U1-U4): every window

'Gold is ruled by the Sun': Sun in a Venus / Mercury / Jupiter nakshatra (rise), Venus, Mercury, Jupiter or a waxing Moon with or aspecting the Sun (hike), Sun in a Saturn / Rahu nakshatra (fall), Saturn or Rahu with or aspecting the Sun (crash). Conjunction = same sign; aspect = whole-sign drishti (Jupiter 5/7/9, Saturn 3/7/10, Rahu 7, so Sun with Ketu counts as Rahu's aspect); waxing = Shukla paksha. These rules disagree with TA Section 34 in places (Pushya is a TA rally star; TA and the 2026 document make malefics with the Sun in Libra bullish): every row carries the conflict note.

Sun-state timeline under your notes (Moon contacts listed separately below)

Each row is a stretch in which the Sun's nakshatra and the planets with or aspecting it stay the same. "Rise : fall" counts the signals of each kind your notes give for that stretch; it is a count, not a weighting. Mercury is never more than ~28 deg from the Sun, so it shares the Sun's sign for much of the year and U2 is active often.

How each row was double-checked

  • Boundaries vs the astronomy layer. Every window start/end was compared with the records written independently by build_2027.py (sign, nakshatra and navamsa ingresses, stations, speed crossings, conjunctions, eclipses, lunations), within 3 minutes. The "Double-check" column names the matching record.
  • Vs the existing calendar. For 2027 rows of the 2026 document's gold rules, the start was compared with the existing calendar's GC rows (15 min; one civil day for combust/rise dates).
  • Vedha engine vs printed rows. The Vedha calculation reproduces all 30 gold-Vedha rows the 2026 document prints for 2026, using the TA Sarvatobhadra wheel and the 2026 speed levels for Mars, Mercury and Venus.
  • Vs the 2026 document's own gold rows. 55 of 62 printed rows checked below are reproduced (table further down).
  • Dated claims in the PDFs. 51 of 58 recomputed (build/pdfref/validation.json), including KD's Saturn-Mars, Venus-Herschel and Mars-speed dates.
  • Day rules (darshan) use New York sunset; combust/rise dates are civil dates (Indian, kalamsha method) as in the existing calendar.

  • Saturn with Jupiter in Sagittarius — TA p.155 [printed 125] Section 34 A #11: does not occur between 20 Nov 2026 and 31 Dec 2027
  • Rahu in Cancer without any benefic — TA p.155 [printed 125] Section 34 A #12: does not occur between 20 Nov 2026 and 31 Dec 2027
  • Jupiter in Aries — TA p.156 [printed 126] Section 34 B #6: does not occur between 20 Nov 2026 and 31 Dec 2027
  • Jupiter in Sagittarius, Capricorn or Aries alone, with benefic or aspected by benefic — TA p.156 [printed 126] Section 34 B #8: does not occur between 20 Nov 2026 and 31 Dec 2027
